# Site Audit — jameskilby.co.uk (v2, deterministic extract)

_Audit date: 2026-06-01. **All 78 of 78** sitemap URLs re-audited. Source data: pre-extracted structured page dumps (curl + BeautifulSoup), not WebFetch._

> **Note on v1.** The first audit reported missing meta description, canonical, and Open Graph tags on most posts. That was a false positive — `WebFetch` uses a small summariser model that did not surface meta tags from the minified single-line HTML reliably. This v2 audit verifies head-tag presence by parsing each page directly. The body-text findings (typos, layout, alt text) are unaffected.

## Head-tag presence (verified by direct parse)

- Meta description present: **78 / 78**
- `rel=canonical` present: **78 / 78**
- Open Graph complete (og:title, og:description, og:image, og:type, og:url): **78 / 78**
- `twitter:card` present: **78 / 78**

No head-tag gaps. The static generator and theme are emitting the full SEO head correctly on every audited page.

## Summary

- Pages audited: **78 of 78**
- Pages with at least one finding: **78**
- Pages clean (no findings): **0**
- Total typos flagged: **463**
- Total cosmetic findings: **561**
- Total SEO findings: **685**

## Systemic issues

Issues appearing on ≥3 pages — likely template/theme/pipeline-level rather than per-post mistakes. Fixing these once should clear a large number of per-page findings.

### Recurring cosmetic patterns
- **(4 pages)** theme chrome rendered as h2 inside the article: '📚 related posts', 'post navigation', 'sim
- **(3 pages)** theme chrome rendered as h2 inside the article ('related posts', 'post navigation', 'simil

### Recurring SEO patterns
- **(7 pages)** theme chrome rendered as h2 inside the article ('related posts', 'post navigation', 'simil
- **(4 pages)** theme chrome rendered as h2 inside the article: 'related posts', 'post navigation', 'simil
- **(4 pages)** theme chrome rendered as h2 inside the article ('📚 related posts', 'post navigation', 'sim
- **(4 pages)** theme chrome ('📚 related posts', 'post navigation', 'similar posts') is emitted as h2 insi
- **(4 pages)** theme chrome ('table of contents', 'related posts', 'post navigation', 'similar posts') is

## Static / Landing pages

### /homelab-software/
**Title:** Homelab - Software - James Kilby _(length: 32)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 428
**One-liner:** Clean, well-structured index page with full SEO metadata; only minor polish opportunities (US 'Optimize' in meta description, generic '→' anchors, light internal linking).

- seo [low]: Meta description uses US spelling 'Optimize' on a UK-English site → Change 'Optimize your digital environment today.' to 'Optimise your digital environment today.' for consistency with UK English used across the rest of the site.
- seo [low]: Title is short and doesn't include the broader search keyword 'self-hosted' → Consider expanding title to something like 'Homelab Software Stack - Self-Hosted Apps - James Kilby' (still under 60 chars) to capture more search intent.
- seo [low]: Generic anchor text on internal links ('See related posts →', 'Read my migration post →', 'Homelab Hardware page →') → Use more descriptive anchor text that includes the topic, e.g. 'Read how I migrated from Pocket to Hoarder' or 'See Semaphore-related posts', to improve link context for users and search engines.
- seo [low]: Page contains many external links but only three internal links to other content on the blog → Add more internal links from individual tools to relevant posts/tags (e.g. Plex, Ollama, Home Assistant, Pi-hole, Traefik) where write-ups exist to strengthen topical linking.
- seo [low]: Open Graph and Twitter descriptions are noticeably shorter and less keyword-rich than the meta description → Expand og:description and twitter:description (currently 'HomeLab - Software provides tools for media, AI, and security.') to use the fuller meta description text for better social previews.

### /vmc/
**Title:** VMware Cloud on AWS (VMC) – Links & Resources - James Kilby _(length: 59)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 386
**One-liner:** Solid resource hub with full meta/OG/Twitter coverage and one H1, but the page is link-heavy with thin prose (386 words), a 'Table of Contents' heading has no TOC under it, one outbound href ends in '.htmlv' (broken), and two list items have inconsistent '( Formerly...' parenthetical formatting.

- typo: "VLSR ( formerly SRM Site Recovery Manager)" → "VLSR (formerly SRM – Site Recovery Manager)" _(context: Inconsistent capitalisation/spacing vs the preceding 'VLCR ( Formerly VCDR...' item; an extra space after the opening parenthesis and a missing separator between 'SRM' and 'Site Recovery Manager'.)_
- typo: "VLCR ( Formerly VCDR VMware Cloud Disaster Recovery)" → "VLCR (formerly VCDR – VMware Cloud Disaster Recovery)" _(context: Stray space after '(', capitalised 'Formerly' (inconsistent with the next line), and missing separator between 'VCDR' and 'VMware Cloud Disaster Recovery'.)_
- typo: "Pre Req’s" → "Prerequisites" _(context: Link text 'Pre Req’s' is informal and grammatically odd ('Req’s' uses an apostrophe to pluralise an abbreviation).)_
- cosmetic [medium]: 'Table of Contents' is rendered as an H2 heading with no actual TOC list underneath — the body shows only the heading text followed by the next section, so the TOC appears empty/non-functional. _(where: ==HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE== / ==BODY== after line 'Table of Contents')_
- cosmetic [low]: Page title 'VMware Cloud on AWS (VMC) – Links & Resources' is duplicated as the first H2 inside the article (it already appears as the H1), creating a redundant heading. _(where: ==HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE== first H2 duplicates the H1)_
- cosmetic [low]: Inconsistent parenthetical style across two consecutive list items: 'VLCR ( Formerly VCDR VMware Cloud Disaster Recovery)' vs 'VLSR ( formerly SRM Site Recovery Manager)' — different capitalisation of 'Formerly/formerly' and both have a stray leading space after '('. _(where: Body under 'VMware Live Recovery' section)_
- cosmetic [low]: Section heading 'Configuration Max ( Current SDDC Version)' has a stray space after the opening parenthesis. _(where: Body under 'Design' section)_
- seo [medium]: Thin content for a hub/resource page (only 386 words of body text; the rest is link list). → Add a short paragraph of context under each major section (Design, Networking, HCX, Aria Integration, Workloads, etc.) explaining what the linked resources cover and when to use them. This gives the page indexable keyword-rich content and improves topical authority.
- seo [medium]: Link 'SPLA Overview' URL ends with '.htmlv' (likely a typo: trailing 'v'). → Fix the href to remove the stray 'v' at the end so the link resolves. Broken outbound links hurt UX and crawl signals.
- seo [medium]: 'Table of Contents' heading is present but no list/links appear under it in the rendered body. → Either remove the 'Table of Contents' heading or populate it with anchor links to each H2 section — currently it is a dangling label that wastes a prime heading slot.
- seo [low]: Anchor text on internal blog links is generic/title-only and the four internal posts are the only on-site links — no contextual sentences surround them. → Wrap the four internal post links ('Using Content Libraries in VMC...', 'Time in a VMC Environment', 'VMC host deep-dive', 'VMC – vSAN ESA') in a one-line description so the anchor text and surrounding text both carry keywords.
- seo [low]: Many H2 sections contain only a single link (e.g., 'Service Status', 'Compliance', 'Networking', 'Microsoft SPLA', 'Performance' subsection 'IDS Dashboard') — heading outline is fragmented. → Consolidate single-link sections under broader parent headings, or add a one-sentence description that explains why the link matters. Reduces outline noise for crawlers and readers.
- seo [low]: Two outbound links labelled 'Release Notes' (one under Design, one under HCX) and two labelled 'Config Max'/'Configuration' point to different products — ambiguous anchor text without surrounding context. → Disambiguate anchors, e.g. 'VMC SDDC Release Notes' and 'HCX 4.11 Release Notes', so each link is self-describing for both users and search engines.
- seo [low]: Page targets the keyword 'VMware Cloud on AWS' but does not link to the broader category/tag archive or other related resource hubs on the site (only four post links). → Add internal links to a VMC category/tag archive and to related hub pages (HCX, Aria, AWS) to spread link equity and help users discover more on-site content.

### /about-me/
**Title:** about me _(length: 8)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 259
**One-liner:** About page has a critically short lowercase <title>, a grammar error in the vExpert/Veeam paragraph, filename-shaped alt on the only image, and several section labels that should be proper headings.

- typo: "I have been a vExpert for the last 12 years and previously and has been honoured to be awarded Veeam Vanguard status for four years." → "I have been a vExpert for the last 12 years and have previously been honoured to be awarded Veeam Vanguard status for four years." _(context: Grammar error: 'and previously and has been honoured' is broken — duplicate 'and', wrong subject agreement ('has' should be 'have').)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Filename-derived alt text on the only content image: alt='55F91C39 F0Dd 42E1 939A Cdffc92C0B52 1024X768' — this is the image caption 'Presenting at the London VMUG with my good friend Gareth' but the alt is the raw filename. _(where: ==IMAGES_IN_ARTICLE== — /wp-content/uploads/2026/02/55f91c39-f0dd-42e1-939a-cdffc92c0b52-1024x768.jpeg)_
- cosmetic [low]: Orphan heading-like line 'Certifications Awarded' appears in body text but is not present in ==HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE==, suggesting it may be rendered as a plain paragraph rather than a proper heading (broken structure). _(where: Body, line between 'Affiliate' section and 'Affiliate Links' section)_
- cosmetic [low]: Orphan section labels 'Affiliate' and 'Connect with me:' appear as standalone paragraphs without being marked up as headings, breaking the visual/structural rhythm of the page. _(where: Body, paragraphs starting 'Affiliate' (line 70) and 'Connect with me:' (line 92))_
- seo [high]: Title tag is only 8 characters ('about me') and lowercase — extremely short and missing keywords/branding. → Use a title around 50-60 chars, e.g. 'About James Kilby — Solution Architect & vExpert' to mirror the og:title and include the author's name as the obvious keyword.
- seo [medium]: <title> is lowercase 'about me' while og:title and twitter:title use proper case 'About Me: Tech Enthusiast & Photographer' — inconsistent branding between SERP and social. → Align the <title> with the Open Graph/Twitter title, capitalised and descriptive.
- seo [medium]: No internal links to other posts/categories on the blog — the About page only links out to vExpert directory, social profiles and affiliate partners. → Add internal links to flagship posts, the homepage, or a /blog/ archive so the About page distributes link equity and helps readers explore the site.
- seo [medium]: Content image has filename-shaped alt ('55F91C39 F0Dd 42E1 939A Cdffc92C0B52 1024X768') which provides zero accessibility/SEO value. → Set descriptive alt text such as 'James Kilby and Gareth presenting at the London VMUG'.
- seo [low]: Thin content — 259 words for an About page is below the recommended 300+ threshold and leaves limited keyword surface for the author's professional bio. → Expand with a short paragraph on professional background, specialisations (VMware, AWS, Veeam, hyperscalers) and notable achievements/links to certifications.
- seo [low]: Heading 'Certifications Awarded' appears as body text rather than as an H2/H3, weakening the page outline (only Disclaimer and Affiliate Links are headings). → Promote 'Certifications Awarded', 'Affiliate' and 'Connect with me' to H2/H3 to improve structure and SEO outline.
- seo [low]: Generic/affiliate-only outbound link list ('Nutmeg', 'Trading212', 'Krisp', etc.) with no nofollow/sponsored indication visible in dump — affiliate links should be marked rel='sponsored' or 'nofollow'. → Confirm rel='sponsored nofollow' on affiliate links to comply with Google's link attribution guidelines.

### /2026/01/using-content-libraries-in-vmc-to-deploy-software-faster/
**Title:** VMC Content Libraries: Faster Software Deployment in SDDC _(length: 57)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 1515
**One-liner:** Solid VMC Content Library walkthrough with strong title/meta/OG; clean up filename-derived image alts, theme-chrome H2s leaking into the article outline, a few sentence fragments, and the 'Content Libary' alt typo.

- typo: "SDDC's" → "SDDCs" _(context: spinning up new SDDC's to test things)_
- typo: "self contained" → "self-contained" _(context: 100% Vanilla and perfectly self contained)_
- typo: "Usually I don't utilise/DX for this" → "Usually I don't utilise DX for this (or 'Direct Connect/DX')" _(context: Usually I don't utilise/DX for this although it will absolutely work over those connections)_
- typo: "would be purely private IP" → "would be purely on private IP (sentence is a fragment)" _(context: work over those connections and would be purely private IP)_
- typo: "public IP's" → "public IPs" _(context: I have a NAT rule on one of my public IP's on my Onprem Watchguard Firewall)_
- typo: "Onprem" → "on-prem" _(context: on my Onprem Watchguard Firewall to connect through to the Onprem vCentre)_
- typo: "outbound https is allowed" → "outbound HTTPS is allowed" _(context: as outbound https is allowed by default)_

- typo: "If you don't have a content library or need to create another one. These are the steps you will need to follow.)_

### /2025/04/warp-the-intelligent-terminal/

- typo: "self taught" → "self-taught" _(context: I am mostly self taught and therefore don't always use best practices — compound adjective needs a hyphen.)_
- typo: "I take some shortcuts that is an acceptable risk" → "I take some shortcuts that are an acceptable risk" _(context: Subject-verb agreement: 'shortcuts' is plural so 'is' should be 'are'.)_
- typo: "Sometimes you just need a helping hand" → "Sometimes you just need a helping hand." _(context: Sentence ends without a full stop before the line break to 'Enter Warp…'.)_
- typo: "Taken from Warps website" → "Taken from Warp's website" _(context: Missing apostrophe on possessive 'Warp's'.)_

- typo: "asked if it was ok to run it which I approved" → "asked if it was OK to run it, which I approved" _(context: 'ok' should be 'OK' and a comma before the non-restrictive 'which' clause.)_
- typo: "Once apt-get had been upgraded the duplicates were no longer in place" → "Once apt-get had been upgraded, the duplicates were no longer in place" _(context: Missing comma after the introductory subordinate clause.)_

### /2018/01/lab-storage/
**Title:** My First Homelab Storage Setup: HP Gen8 & Xpenology - James Kilby _(length: 65)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 791
**One-liner:** Short 2018 post about a Gen8/Xpenology homelab build with solid title, canonical and OG plumbing, but a weak generic meta/OG description, thin body content, a stale 'pics coming' promise, several small typos (Useable, Nics, Red's, ISO's), and theme-chrome H2s plus a raw '&#038;' entity bleeding into the article.

- typo: "I also have a DS216+II Synology with 2x 2TB WD Red’s this is my tier 2 lab storage. for backups ISO’s etc" → "I also have a DS216+II Synology with 2x 2TB WD Reds; this is my tier 2 lab storage, for backups, ISOs etc." _(context: Run-on sentence with comma splice, lowercase 'for' starting a new sentence fragment, and stray apostrophes in plurals (Red's/ISO's).)_
- typo: "The 2xWD Red’s are in an SHR configuration with the SSDs running as an SSD read-write cache" → "The 2x WD Reds are in an SHR configuration with the SSDs running as an SSD read-write cache." _(context: Greengrocer's apostrophe in 'Red’s' (should be plural 'Reds'), and the sentence is missing a terminating full stop.)_
- typo: "This gives me 2.6TB Useable" → "This gives me 2.6TB usable" _(context: 'Useable' should be lowercase mid-sentence; standard UK spelling is 'usable'.)_
- typo: "I have the 4 Nics split into two LACP bonds." → "I have the 4 NICs split into two LACP bonds." _(context: 'Nics' should be capitalised as 'NICs' (Network Interface Cards) for consistency with '4x 1Gb/s NIC' above.)_
- typo: "I will add some pics when I have tidied up the lab/cables" → "I will add some pics when I have tidied up the lab/cables." _(context: Sentence is missing a terminating full stop.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Raw HTML entity '&#038;' rendered as text instead of an ampersand in the related-posts link 'Homelab Storage Upgrade: Synology DS918 for VMware &#038; NFS'. _(where: Related Posts list / BODY line referencing 'VMware &#038; NFS')_
- cosmetic [medium]: Theme chrome rendered inside the article: 'Connect with me:', '📚 Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts' all appear as part of the post body, plus duplicated 'Read More ... Continue' blocks for each related post. _(where: End of BODY (everything from 'Connect with me:' onwards))_
- cosmetic [medium]: Each related post block is duplicated — once as a flattened single-line block (categories + title + author + dates + excerpt + 'Read More') and again as title + excerpt + 'Read More'. Reads as repeated content. _(where: Similar Posts section in BODY)_
- cosmetic [low]: Specs are formatted as a series of single-line paragraphs ('1x INTEL Core i3-4130...', '16Gb of RAM', '4x 1Gb/s NIC', etc.) rather than a proper bulleted list — looks like a broken/converted list. _(where: BODY 'Currently Configured:' section)_
- cosmetic [low]: Inconsistent units: 'Gb' is used where 'GB' is meant ('16Gb of RAM', '80GB' later) — 'Gb' is gigabits and visually inconsistent within the spec block. _(where: BODY spec list)_
- seo [high]: Meta description is the lazy opening line of the post ('I have been meaning to post around some of the lab setup for a while...') and contains no target keywords like 'HP Gen8', 'Xpenology', 'homelab storage' or 'Synology'. → Rewrite to 120-158 chars including primary keywords, e.g. 'My first homelab storage build: an HP Gen8 Microserver running Xpenology with WD Red drives, SSD read/write cache, and a Synology DS216+II tier-2 NAS.'
- seo [high]: Open Graph and Twitter title/description are generic ('Lab Storage: Optimize Your Setup' / 'Check out my lab storage configuration and tips!') and don't match the page title or describe the HP Gen8 / Xpenology build. → Set og:title and twitter:title to match the post title ('My First Homelab Storage Setup: HP Gen8 & Xpenology'), and og/twitter:description to the same descriptive sentence as the meta description.
- seo [high]: Thin content — only 791 words in the dump and the actual post body (above 'Connect with me:') is roughly 200 words. Most of the page is theme chrome / related-post duplication. → Expand the post with a parts/diagram of the Gen8 build, BIOS/iLO notes, Xpenology version + bootloader used in 2018, SHR cache configuration steps, performance numbers, and lessons learned — aim for 600+ words of actual body.
- seo [medium]: Theme chrome rendered as H2s inside the article ('📚 Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') dilutes the heading outline and the article has zero content H2s of its own. → Demote related-posts/post-navigation chrome below H2 (or move outside <article>), and add real content H2s such as 'Hardware', 'Xpenology configuration', 'Storage layout (SHR + SSD cache)', 'Tier 2: Synology DS216+II', 'Apps & containers'.
- seo [medium]: Internal links are almost entirely auto-generated 'Related Posts' / 'Similar Posts' theme blocks — the actual hand-written body has no contextual links to follow-up posts like /2019/02/lab-storage-2/ or the SSD failure post. → Add 1-2 in-body contextual links, e.g. 'I later upgraded this setup — see Homelab Storage Upgrade: Synology DS918 for VMware & NFS' and link to the SSD failure post inline.
- seo [medium]: Stale/dangling promise: 'I will add some pics when I have tidied up the lab/cables' — written in January 2018 and the pics were never added; the page still has no body images. → Either add the photos now or remove the sentence so readers aren't left waiting on content that never arrived.
- seo [low]: Image alt text for related-post thumbnails is just the post title (e.g. 'Forcing an Upgrade to vSphere 8', 'Homelab SSD Failure: How Synology RAID Saved My Data', 'Starlink Satellite Internet Review: Rural Broadband Solution') — and crucially the article itself has zero in-body content images. → Add one or two original in-body images (Gen8 chassis, drive bays, SHR config screenshot) with descriptive alt text — this also helps with the thin-content issue.
- seo [low]: Generic anchor text in the related-posts area: repeated 'Read More ... Continue' and a 'Can you really squeeze 96TB in 1U ?' link with trailing space before the question mark. → Clean up the related-post link template to avoid 'Read More <title> Continue' duplication and fix the spacing before '?'.

### /2018/05/aws-status-page-monitoring-included/
**Title:** AWS Status Page - Monitoring Included - James Kilby _(length: 51)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 698
**One-liner:** Short 2018 post with a clean title/meta/canonical/OG set, but thin actual content (~110 words once chrome is removed), filename-shaped alt text, raw HTML entities in Related Posts anchors, H2->H4 heading skip, and 'here'/'here.' as the only descriptive anchors on its two key external links.

- typo: "The tool I deployed lambstatus supports pulling metrics" → "The tool I deployed, lambstatus, supports pulling metrics" _(context: Missing commas around the appositive 'lambstatus' in the first paragraph.)_
- typo: "at effectively zero cost" → "at effectively zero cost." _(context: The paragraph ends without a full stop: 'effectively zero cost'.)_
- typo: "is documented in the Git repo here" → "is documented in the Git repo here." _(context: Sentence under 'Site Response' lacks a full stop at the end.)_
- cosmetic [high]: Theme chrome leaked into article copy: 'Docker | Homelab | Hosting | Kubernetes Use Portainer in a Homelab with GitHub By James December 9, 2022 April 16, 2026 Late to the party...' — categories, author, dates and excerpt are concatenated as a single run-on paragraph before each Similar Post card. _(where: Body section, 'Similar Posts' block (repeats for every related card).)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Raw HTML entities rendered as text in related-post link titles: '&#038;' (ampersand) and '&#8211;' (en dash) appear in anchor text instead of decoded characters. _(where: Related Posts list: 'Monitoring VMware Cloud on AWS: Tools &#038; Approaches (Part 1)', 'AWS Solution Architect &#8211; Associate', 'AWS For Beginners: IAM Setup, Root Security &#038; Billing Alerts'.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Filename-derived alt text on the hero/lead image: alt='Amazonwebservices Logo.Svg'. _(where: First image: /wp-content/uploads/2018/05/AmazonWebservices_Logo.svg_.png)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Filename-derived alt text on the screenshot: alt='Screen Shot 2018 05 15 at 22.39.01'. _(where: Second image: /wp-content/uploads/2023/04/Screen-Shot-2018-05-15-at-22.39.01.png)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Each Similar Posts card duplicates its title, excerpt and 'Read More ... Continue' link twice in body text. _(where: Similar Posts section: every card title appears as both inline run-on and standalone heading/excerpt.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: 'Read More <title> Continue' phrasing is a stray template artefact appearing repeatedly as visible body text. _(where: Every Similar Posts entry.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Stale future-dated 'April 16, 2026' modified date stamped on every Similar Posts card despite original publish dates (e.g., 2019, 2022) — visible to readers. _(where: Similar Posts cards.)_
- seo [high]: Thin content: ~110 words of actual post body (the 698 wordcount is inflated by Similar Posts/Related Posts chrome). → Expand the post with the actual Lambda/CloudWatch monitoring setup steps, polling interval rationale, sample code, and a result screenshot caption — or consolidate with a related AWS post.
- seo [medium]: Multiple theme-chrome H2s inside the article ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') dilute the document outline; only one true content H2 ('AWS Status Page – Enhancements') exists. → Render related/post-navigation/similar-posts blocks outside <article>/<main> or downgrade their headings so the article outline reflects content only.
- seo [medium]: Heading hierarchy skips from H2 ('AWS Status Page – Enhancements') directly to H4 ('Site Response'). → Promote 'Site Response' to H3 for a clean H1 -> H2 -> H3 outline.
- seo [medium]: Generic anchor text on the two most important external links: 'here.' (lambstatus demo) and 'here' (set-up guide). → Replace with descriptive anchors such as 'live status page demo' and 'Lambstatus custom HTTP/S monitoring guide'.
- seo [medium]: Hero image alt text is filename-derived ('Amazonwebservices Logo.Svg') and the screenshot alt is also filename-derived ('Screen Shot 2018 05 15 at 22.39.01'). → Rewrite alt text descriptively, e.g., 'AWS logo' and 'Lambstatus status page showing site response time graph from CloudWatch'.
- seo [low]: Meta description duplicates the opening sentence verbatim and ends abruptly without a call-to-action or core keyword 'CloudWatch monitoring'. → Rewrite meta description (120-158 chars) summarising the post: e.g., 'How I added AWS CloudWatch response-time monitoring to a Lambstatus status page using a one-minute Lambda poll — guide, costs and results.'
- seo [low]: Open Graph / Twitter title and description differ from the page <title> and meta description ('Real-Time Monitoring Insights' / 'Stay updated with AWS service metrics. Check the status now!'), which reads like generic AI marketing copy and doesn't match the actual content (a personal Lambstatus + CloudWatch experiment). → Align og:title / twitter:title with the page title and rewrite og:description to describe the actual content.
- seo [low]: No internal links to other posts in the body copy (only the auto-generated Related/Similar Posts widgets). → Add a contextual link to the earlier 'AWS Status Page' setup post and to relevant Lambda/CloudWatch posts from within the prose.
- seo [low]: Stale time reference: 'running without a hitch for nearly a month now' on a 2018 post — readers landing in 2026 see this as out-of-date and unverified. → Add a dated update line confirming current status (still running / decommissioned) so the post does not appear abandoned.

### /2018/06/nutanix-command-reference-guide/
**Title:** Nutanix Command Reference Guide - James Kilby _(length: 45)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 487
**One-liner:** Tiny (under 150 words of real content) cheat-sheet post marred by three grammar slips in the intro/meta ('Its', 'i', 'ill'), an en-dash where a CLI double-hyphen belongs, and a Similar Posts block that duplicates every entry and leaks '&#038;' entities.

- typo: "Its here as a reference" → "It's here as a reference" _(context: Intro sentence: 'This is a list of Nutanix commands I have found useful. Its here as a reference...' — missing apostrophe; should be 'It's'.)_
- typo: "if i need a command" → "if I need a command" _(context: Intro sentence: '...if i need a command more than a few times ill generally add it here.' — pronoun 'i' should be capitalised.)_
- typo: "ill generally add it here" → "I'll generally add it here" _(context: Intro sentence: '...more than a few times ill generally add it here.' — 'ill' should be the contraction 'I'll'.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: HTML entity '&#038;' rendered as literal text in Related Posts titles (should display as '&') _(where: Related Posts block: 'New Homelab Nodes: SuperMicro BigTwin for VMware &#038; Nutanix' etc.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Theme chrome ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts', 'Connect with me') rendered inside the article body, polluting the post content _(where: End of BODY — Similar Posts block duplicates each related post twice (category line + summary + 'Read More ... Continue').)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Duplicated related-post entries: each Similar Post appears twice (once as a one-line summary line, then again as title + excerpt + 'Read More ... Continue') _(where: BODY lines 125-147 — Similar Posts section.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Inconsistent en-dash/hyphen in CLI snippet 'ncc health_checks run_all –parallel=4' uses an en-dash (–) where '--parallel=4' (double hyphen) would be the correct CLI flag _(where: CLI section: 'ncc health_checks run_all –parallel=4 . ( 4 is the max number)')_
- cosmetic [low]: Stray 'Previous Previous' and 'Next Continue' anchor text from post navigation _(where: Post navigation links: 'Previous Previous AWS Status Page – Monitoring Included' / 'Next Continue And now for something completely different'.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Hero image alt text is filename-derived: 'Nutanix Logo Hi Rez Reverse W Carrier' _(where: ==IMAGES_IN_ARTICLE== first image alt attribute.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Stray trailing ' . ' / ' .' tokens after commands look like leftover punctuation noise _(where: CLI section: 'ncc health_checks run_all –parallel=4 .' and 'curl localhost:2019/prism/leader .')_
- cosmetic [low]: Heading hierarchy skip: H2 'Nutanix Command List' jumps directly to H4 ('CLI', 'WEB') with no intervening H3 _(where: ==HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE== — H2 -> H4 skip.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Theme chrome rendered as H2 inside the article ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') _(where: ==HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE== — three H2s after the post content.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Empty/decorative anchor entries '<no text>' appearing in Related Posts links (likely image-only links missing aria-label) _(where: ==LINKS_IN_ARTICLE== — three '<no text>' -> related post URLs.)_
- cosmetic [low]: Stray 'Connect with me:' label appears as plain text with no following social links rendered in body _(where: BODY line 113 — 'Connect with me:' sits alone before Related Posts.)_
- seo [high]: Thin content: only 487 words including theme chrome; the actual post body is under 150 words → Expand each command with a short explanation of when/why to use it, expected output, and any caveats (RF, AOS version). Add an intro paragraph with target keywords.
- seo [medium]: Meta description starts with weak phrasing and contains the same typos as the body ('Its here', 'if i need', 'ill generally add') → Rewrite meta description to be polished and keyword-rich, e.g. 'A quick-reference list of useful Nutanix CLI and web commands — ncli, ncc, curator and CVM shutdown — collected from real-world admin work.' (around 140-155 chars).
- seo [medium]: Few internal links to other on-site Nutanix posts inside the body — the only internal post links come from the auto-generated Related/Similar Posts modules → Add 1-2 contextual in-body links to related Nutanix posts (e.g. NCP exam post, Nutanix CE at home) with descriptive anchor text.
- seo [low]: Heading hierarchy skip H2 -> H4 (CLI, WEB) under 'Nutanix Command List' → Promote 'CLI' and 'WEB' to H3 so the outline reads H2 -> H3 instead of skipping a level.
- seo [low]: Theme chrome ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') rendered as H2 inside the article dilutes the outline → Render these section labels outside <article>/<main>, or downgrade them to non-heading elements (div + visually styled label).
- seo [low]: Hero image alt text is filename-derived ('Nutanix Logo Hi Rez Reverse W Carrier') → Set a meaningful alt like 'Nutanix logo' or leave empty if purely decorative.
- seo [low]: Generic navigation anchor text 'Previous Previous ...' / 'Next Continue ...' and three '<no text>' image links → Clean up duplicated 'Previous'/'Next' labels in the theme, and add aria-label or alt text to the image-only related-post thumbnails.
- seo [low]: Title is short (45 chars) and omits a high-intent keyword like 'CLI' or 'ncli' → Consider 'Nutanix CLI Command Reference Guide (ncli, ncc, curator) - James Kilby' or similar to capture 'Nutanix CLI' search intent.

### /2018/10/and-now-for-something-completely-different/
**Title:** And now for something completely different - James Kilby _(length: 56)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 784
**One-liner:** Clean short personal post; main wins are aligning og:title with page title, fixing the truncated meta description, replacing the 'Fwbxyba7' filename alt, and decoding the '&#8211;' entity in Related Posts.

- typo: "I have been lucky enough to be involved with all of these ( some much more than others) Although the work is never complete Zen are in a good place." → "I have been lucky enough to be involved with all of these (some much more than others). Although the work is never complete, Zen are in a good place." _(context: Missing period before 'Although', stray space inside parenthesis, missing comma after 'complete'.)_
- typo: "for professional services in 2018" → "for professional services in 2018." _(context: Sentence ending 'awarded both the EMEA and Global VMware Partner Innovation Award for professional services in 2018' is missing a full stop.)_
- typo: "one or two others….." → "one or two others..." _(context: Five dots instead of a standard ellipsis (three dots) at end of final paragraph.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Raw HTML entity '&#8211;' rendered as literal text in Related Posts list ('VMware &#8211; Going out with a Bang!') instead of an en-dash. _(where: Body, Related Posts section)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Filename-derived alt text 'Fwbxyba7' on the post's featured/inline image (fWbXybA7.png). _(where: IMAGES_IN_ARTICLE, first image)_
- cosmetic [low]: Theme chrome ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') rendered as H2 inside the article, diluting the document outline. _(where: HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE)_
- cosmetic [low]: Unclosed parenthesis in Similar Posts excerpt: 'I recently sat (and passed the VMware HCI Master Specialist exam (5V0-21.' — truncated mid-sentence and missing closing brackets. _(where: Body, Similar Posts (VMware Certified Master Specialist HCI 2020 excerpt))_
- cosmetic [low]: Similar Posts cards show duplicated/concatenated metadata strings like 'Read More ... Continue' repeated twice per card. _(where: Body, Similar Posts block)_
- cosmetic [low]: Several Similar Posts cards display 'April 16, 2026' as a second date alongside the original publication date — looks like a stale 'modified' timestamp shown to readers. _(where: Body, Similar Posts cards)_
- seo [medium]: Meta description is truncated mid-sentence ('...what was originally a customer-focused role') with no full stop and no keyword payoff. → Rewrite to 120-158 chars with a complete sentence that mentions Zen Internet, the role change to Xtravirt, and VMware consulting.
- seo [medium]: Title and og:title disagree — page <title> is 'And now for something completely different' while og:title is 'My Journey at Zen Internet: A Role Transformation'. Social shares will look unrelated to the on-page title. → Align og:title with the page title (or vice versa) so the canonical headline is consistent across surfaces.
- seo [medium]: Title contains no search keywords (no mention of Xtravirt, Zen Internet, VMware, career change). → Consider a subtitle pattern, e.g. 'And now for something completely different - Joining Xtravirt as a Senior Consultant'.
- seo [medium]: Featured image has filename-derived alt 'Fwbxyba7' which provides no descriptive value. → Replace with a descriptive alt such as 'Xtravirt logo' or similar relating to the announcement.
- seo [low]: Theme chrome headings ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') sit as H2 inside the article, breaking the semantic outline. → Move these blocks outside <article>/<main> or demote them so the article outline reflects post content only.
- seo [low]: Empty link anchors ('<no text>') on Similar Posts thumbnail links offer no context to crawlers or screen readers. → Add aria-label or visually hidden text describing the linked post.
- seo [low]: Related Posts anchor 'VMware &#8211; Going out with a Bang!' shows a raw HTML entity instead of an en-dash. → Decode HTML entities when rendering Related Posts titles.
- seo [low]: Stale reference: 'awarded both the EMEA and Global VMware Partner Innovation Award ... in 2018' is fine, but no internal link to a later Xtravirt-era post or follow-up exists in the body — only chrome-generated Similar Posts. → Add an in-body internal link from this announcement to a later post reflecting on the move (e.g. a subsequent Xtravirt/VMware piece).
- seo [low]: Wordpress category label rendered as 'Wordpress' rather than the brand's correct casing 'WordPress'. → Update category display name to 'WordPress'.

### /2018/12/new-laptop/
**Title:** MacBook Air 2018 Retina Review: My Setup & Build Scripts - James Kilby _(length: 70)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 278
**One-liner:** Short 2018 MacBook Air review with several small grammar slips, a thin 278-word body that promises but doesn't show build scripts, a too-short meta description, and theme-chrome H2s plus a duplicated 'Related Posts' block cluttering the outline.

- typo: "MacBook Pro’s weren’t worth it" → "MacBook Pros weren’t worth it" _(context: decided the MacBook Pro’s weren’t worth it — incorrect apostrophe; plural Pros should not take an apostrophe.)_
- typo: "another MacBook Air ." → "another MacBook Air." _(context: yes you guessed it another MacBook Air . — stray space before the full stop.)_
- typo: "yes you guessed it another MacBook Air" → "yes, you guessed it, another MacBook Air" _(context: So I have replaced my Air with yes you guessed it another MacBook Air — missing commas around the parenthetical.)_
- typo: "T2 Security chip" → "T2 Security chip." _(context: It also has the security benefits of the T2 Security chip — sentence ends without a full stop.)_
- typo: "16Gb of RAM" → "16GB of RAM" _(context: the retina version with 16Gb of RAM and 512GB PCIe-based SSD — inconsistent capitalisation (Gb means gigabits; should be GB to match 512GB).)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Raw HTML entity '&#038;' rendered as text in related-post link anchor ('Speed Up iOS &#038; Mac Updates') instead of an ampersand _(where: Body / Related Posts link)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Theme chrome rendered as H2 inside the article: '📚 Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts' — dilutes the article outline _(where: HEADINGS_IN_ARTICLE)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Duplicated related-post block — 'How Apple Content Caching Works' heading and intro paragraph appear three times in the body (Related Posts, Similar Posts card, and again as a standalone heading) _(where: Body, after 'Connect with me:')_
- cosmetic [medium]: Run-on related-post card text: 'Apple How Apple Content Caching Works: Speed Up iOS & Mac Updates By James February 8, 2021 April 16, 2026 I have slowly morphed…' — no separators between category, title, author, dates and excerpt _(where: Similar Posts block)_
- cosmetic [medium]: Empty anchor text link ('<no text>' -> /2021/02/apple-content-caching/) — likely an unlabelled image or icon link with no accessible name _(where: LINKS_IN_ARTICLE)_
- cosmetic [low]: Hero image alt text 'Colorware' is just the filename/brand and gives no context about the MacBook being reviewed _(where: IMAGES_IN_ARTICLE /wp-content/uploads/2018/12/colorware.jpg)_
- seo [medium]: Meta description is only 92 characters and ends mid-thought ('…back in 2011.') — wastes available SERP real estate and contains no keywords like 'MacBook Air', 'review', 'build scripts' → Rewrite to 140-158 chars including key terms, e.g. 'My review of the 2018 Retina MacBook Air (16GB / 512GB) plus the Mac build scripts I use to set up a new machine quickly.'
- seo [medium]: Thin content — 278 words of actual post copy (below the 300-word threshold) for a review/build-scripts post → Expand with the actual build script content, spec table, pros/cons, and a few months of real-world use notes.
- seo [medium]: No internal links in the body to related posts (e.g. the earlier 2011 MacBook Air mention, the EDC backpack follow-up, or other Apple posts) — only the auto-generated 'Related/Similar' chrome links out → Add inline contextual links to /category/apple/ posts and the 'What's In My Tech Bag' follow-up directly from the prose.
- seo [medium]: Stale promise — 'They are available below' but the build scripts are not visible in the extracted body → Either embed/link the scripts (GitHub gist or repo) directly under that sentence, or remove the promise.
- seo [low]: Title length is exactly 70 characters — at the upper truncation limit in SERPs once the site suffix is appended → Trim the suffix or shorten, e.g. 'MacBook Air 2018 Retina Review & My Mac Build Scripts' to leave more headroom.
- seo [low]: OG/Twitter title ('New MacBook Air Review: Lightweight & Powerful') and page <title> differ significantly, which can confuse social previews vs SERP → Align them around a consistent keyword phrase (MacBook Air 2018 Retina Review).
- seo [low]: Hero image alt 'Colorware' is not descriptive of the post topic and misses a keyword opportunity → Use descriptive alt such as 'Retina MacBook Air 2018 on a desk' (or whatever the photo shows).
- seo [low]: Theme chrome ('Related Posts', 'Post navigation', 'Similar Posts') rendered as H2 inside the article competes with the article's own outline for SEO weight → Demote chrome headings to H3/H4 or move them outside the <article>/<main> landmark.
- seo [low]: Generic anchor text on category/tag/social links ('Apple', '# Apple', '# Brew', 'GitHub', 'Twitter', 'LinkedIn') — fine for chrome but the in-body 'Apple' category link is the only contextual link and adds no keyword value → Add descriptive inline links inside the prose (e.g. 'my 2011 MacBook Air post').

### /2017/05/money-saving-uk-version/
**Title:** UK Money Saving Tips: Banking, Rewards & Travel Cards - James Kilby _(length: 67)_  |  **H1 count:** 1  |  **Words:** 804
**One-liner:** Decent structure and complete metadata, but the meta description is off-topic (talks about a Veeam conference instead of money-saving), 2017 rates are stale, promised parts 2 and 3 aren't linked, and bank sections lack H2 subheadings.

- typo: "First direct give you a good bonus" → "First Direct gives you a good bonus" _(context: Inconsistent capitalisation ('First direct' vs the heading 'First Direct') and subject-verb agreement: a single bank should take 'gives'.)_
- typo: "Barclays mortgage , and £1" → "Barclays mortgage, and £1" _(context: Stray space before the comma after 'Barclays mortgage'.)_
- typo: "I have a number of UK current accounts" → "I have a number of UK current accounts." _(context: Sentence 'I currently have a number of UK current accounts' is missing a terminal full stop before the next paragraph.)_
- cosmetic [medium]: First long paragraph about Barclays Bank mashes a numbered list (1./2./3.) into one block rather than rendering as a proper list; later items 2. and 3. then appear as separate paragraphs, breaking the list's visual continuity. _(where: BODY: 'Barclays Bank (Main Account)...' paragraph and following '2.'/'3.' paragraphs)_
- cosmetic [low]: Bare arithmetic line '156+125+162.50+15+135+97.50+150' is shown as raw text with no formatting or explanation of what each figure represents. _(where: BODY: 'Total savings for current/regular saving' section)_
- cosmetic [low]: Body ends abruptly at 'Connect with me:' with no closing sentence or link to the follow-up posts promised in the intro (parts 2 and 3). _(where: BODY: final line)_
- seo [high]: Meta description is the article's opening sentence about a New Orleans conference and Veeam Vanguard - it does not mention banking, savings, rewards or travel cards, so it fails to reinforce the page's actual topic or keywords. → Write a bespoke 140-155 char meta description summarising the UK banking, rewards and travel card tips covered (e.g. 'Practical UK money-saving tips: best current accounts, regular savers, cashback rewards and credit-card deals to cut banking and travel costs.').
- seo [medium]: Promised follow-up posts ('2. Maximising credit card deals' and '3. Minimising costs when travelling') are referenced but there are no internal links to those posts, leaving the series disconnected. → Add internal links from the numbered intro list to the credit-card and travel posts if they exist (or remove/update the promise if they were never published).
- seo [medium]: Only internal link is the 'Money' category; there are no contextual internal links to related posts on banking, rewards, travel or finance. → Add 2-3 contextual internal links to related posts (e.g. Veeam Vanguard recap, credit card / travel money posts) to improve crawl depth and topical clustering.
- seo [medium]: Content is from May 2017 and quotes very specific 2017 interest rates and rewards (Nationwide FlexDirect 5% on £2,500, Barclays Blue Rewards £7+£3+£5+£1 structure, TSB Classic Plus 3% / £5 reward, First Direct £150 switch bonus) that have all changed substantially. → Add a dated 'Last updated' note or editorial warning that rates/rewards reflect 2017 and may now be inaccurate, or refresh figures and republish.
- seo [medium]: Article has no images at all (0 in IMAGES_IN_ARTICLE), no headings beyond H1, and uses bank names as plain paragraph leads rather than H2/H3 subheadings - hurts scannability and on-page SEO outline. → Promote each bank section ('Barclays Bank', 'Nationwide FlexDirect', 'Nationwide FlexPlus', 'TSB Classic Plus', 'First Direct 1st Account') to H2 subheadings and consider adding at least one relevant image with descriptive alt text.
- seo [low]: Title length is 67 characters, slightly above the ideal 50-60 range and likely to be truncated in SERPs. → Trim to ~60 chars, e.g. 'UK Money Saving Tips: Banking, Rewards & Travel Cards'.
- seo [low]: Open Graph / Twitter title and description ('Maximize Your Savings with UK Banking Tips' / 'Unlock smart banking strategies to save more now!') use US spelling 'Maximize' and clickbait phrasing inconsistent with the on-page UK English title. → Align OG/Twitter title with the on-page title using UK spelling ('Maximise...') and a description that matches the actual content.
- seo [low]: Body uses 'YMMV' acronym without expansion and includes vague generic phrasing rather than keyword-rich language around 'UK current accounts', 'cashback', 'switching bonus' etc. → Expand acronyms once and weave target keywords (UK current account, switching bonus, regular saver, cashback rewards) naturally into the intro and section leads.
